````tsx
      { name: 'Kalomai Park Resort & Urbanizaciones', acronym: 'KALOMAI', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/kalomaipark/', initial: 'K', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#059669,#10B981)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_kalomai.webp', subcategory: 'urbanizaciones' },
      { name: 'Mar Adentro Urbanización & Crystal Lagoons', acronym: 'MAR ADENTRO', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/MarAdentroBolivia/', initial: 'M', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#0284C7,#38BDF8)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_mar_adentro.webp', subcategory: 'urbanizaciones' },
      { name: 'Condado del Valle Urbanizaciones', acronym: 'CONDADO DEL VALLE', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/Condadodelvallebolivia/', initial: 'C', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#B45309,#F59E0B)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_condado_del_valle.webp', subcategory: 'urbanizaciones' },
      { name: 'Colinas del Urubó Condominio Privado', acronym: 'COLINAS DEL URUBÓ', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/colinasdelurubo/', initial: 'C', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#15803D,#22C55E)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_colinas_del_urubo.webp', subcategory: 'urbanizaciones' },
      { name: 'Lotes al Día — Venta de Terrenos en Cuotas', acronym: 'LOTES AL DÍA', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/lotesaldia/', initial: 'L', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#B91C1C,#EF4444)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_terrenos_al_dia.webp', subcategory: 'lotes' },
      { name: 'Inbolsa — Terrenos & Lotes Urbanos', acronym: 'INBOLSA LOTES', url: 'https://www.inbolsa.com/', initial: 'I', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#4338CA,#6366F1)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_inbolsa.webp', subcategory: 'lotes' },
      { name: 'Lotes y Urbanizaciones La Paz - El Alto', acronym: 'LOTES LA PAZ', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/terrenoselaltoypaz/', initial: 'L', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#D97706,#FBBF24)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_lotes_la_paz.webp', subcategory: 'lotes' },
      { name: 'TerraVida — Lotes & Condominios Ecológicos', acronym: 'TERRAVIDA', url: 'https://www.facebook.com/terravidabolivia/', initial: 'T', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#047857,#34D399)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_terravida.webp', subcategory: 'lotes' },
      { name: 'Smart Studio Desarrollos Inmobiliarios', acronym: 'SMART STUDIO', url: 'https://www.smartstudio.com.bo/', initial: 'S', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#7C3AED,#A78BFA)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_smart_studio.webp', subcategory: 'inmobiliarias' },
      { name: 'Latin Bienes Raíces Bolivia', acronym: 'LATIN BIENES', url: 'https://www.latinbienesraices.com/', initial: 'L', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#0369A1,#0EA5E9)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_latin_bienes_raices.webp', subcategory: 'inmobiliarias' },
      { name: 'Bolivia Propiedades Portal Inmobiliario', acronym: 'BOLIVIA PROPIEDADES', url: 'https://www.boliviapropiedades.com/', initial: 'B', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#BE185D,#F43F5E)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_bolivia_propiedades.webp', subcategory: 'inmobiliarias' },
      { name: 'Casa Crédito Inmobiliaria Bolivia', acronym: 'CASA CRÉDITO', url: 'https://casacredito.com/bolivia/', initial: 'C', gradient: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#1D4ED8,#3B82F6)', logo: '/assets/logos/urbanizaciones_casa_credito.webp', subcategory: 'inmobiliarias' },
    ],
  },
];

const PILLS = [
  { id: 'all', label: `Todos los rubros (${RUBROS.length})` },
  { id: 'urbanizaciones', label: 'Urbanizaciones & Inmobiliarias' },
  { id: 'obra-gruesa', label: 'Obra Gruesa & Estructura' },
  { id: 'maquinaria', label: 'Maquinaria & Herramientas' },
  { id: 'acabados', label: 'Acabados & Decoración' },
  { id: 'tecnologia', label: 'Tecnología & Seguridad' },
];

const FILTER_MAP: Record<string, string[]> = {
  urbanizaciones: ['urbanizaciones'],
  'obra-gruesa': ['cemento', 'ladrillo', 'fierro', 'estuco', 'pegamentos'],
  maquinaria: ['maquinaria-pesada', 'carpinteria', 'cerrajeria', 'estanteria-industrial'],
  acabados: [
    'pintura',
    'ceramica',
    'tableros-melaminicos',
    'aluminio',
    'decoracion-de-interior',
    'paisajismo',
    'vidrio',
    'techos',
    'pisos',
    'equipamiento-de-cocina',
    'banos-sanitarios-griferia',
    'marcos-de-ventana',
  ],
  tecnologia: ['materiales-electricos', 'domotica', 'seguridad'],
};

type UrbanSubtab = 'all' | 'urbanizaciones' | 'lotes' | 'inmobiliarias';

/** Minúsculas y sin tildes: "cordoba" encuentra "Córdoba", "ceramica" encuentra "Cerámica". */
const normalize = (text: string) =>
  text.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();

export default function RubrosDirectory() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [activePill, setActivePill] = useState('all');
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [urbanSubtab, setUrbanSubtab] = useState<UrbanSubtab>('all');

  const isSearching = query.trim() !== '';

  // Cada rubro con SOLO las empresas que corresponden a la búsqueda y a la pestaña activa
  const visibleRubros = useMemo(() => {
    const q = normalize(query);
    const allowed = activePill !== 'all' ? FILTER_MAP[activePill] : null;

    return RUBROS.filter((r) => !allowed || allowed.includes(r.id))
      .map((rubro) => {
        let companies = rubro.companies;

        if (q) {
          const rubroMatches = normalize(rubro.name).includes(q);
          if (!rubroMatches) {
            companies = companies.filter(
              (c) => normalize(c.name).includes(q) || normalize(c.acronym).includes(q),
            );
          }
        }

        if (rubro.id === 'urbanizaciones' && urbanSubtab !== 'all') {
          companies = companies.filter((c) => c.subcategory === urbanSubtab);
        }

        return { rubro, companies };
      })
      .filter(({ rubro, companies }) => !q || companies.length > 0 || normalize(rubro.name).includes(q));
  }, [query, activePill, urbanSubtab]);

  return (
    <section className="rubros" id="rubros">
      <div className="wrap">
        <motion.div
          className="section-head"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
          whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          viewport={{ once: true }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
        >
          <span className="section-tag">Rubros expositores</span>
          <h2>Todo lo que se construye, en un solo pabellón</h2>
          <p>
            Los rubros líderes conforman la oferta integral de esta edición - desde
            urbanizaciones, lotes y maquinaria pesada hasta los acabados más finos.
          </p>
        </motion.div>

        <div className="rubros-controls-wrap">
          <div className="rubros-search-container relative flex items-center">
            <Search className="rubros-search-icon absolute left-4 text-gray-400" size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
            <input
              type="search"
              className="rubros-search-input"
              placeholder="Buscar rubro, empresa o producto..."
              aria-label="Buscar rubro, empresa o producto"
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            />
            {query && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="rubros-clear-btn absolute right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-black"
                onClick={() => setQuery('')}
                aria-label="Borrar búsqueda"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="rubros-pills-row">
            {PILLS.map((pill) => (
              <button
                key={pill.id}
                type="button"
                className={`rubro-pill ${activePill === pill.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                aria-pressed={activePill === pill.id}
                onClick={() => setActivePill(pill.id)}
              >
                {pill.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rubros-accordion">
          {visibleRubros.length === 0 && (
            <div className="companies-empty" role="status">
              <p>No se encontraron rubros ni empresas para &ldquo;{query.trim()}&rdquo;.</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {visibleRubros.map(({ rubro, companies }) => {
            const isOpen = openId === rubro.id || isSearching;
            const bodyId = `rubro-body-${rubro.id}`;
            const countLabel =
              rubro.companies.length > 0
                ? `${companies.length} empresa${companies.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`
                : 'Próximamente';
            const countOf = (sub: UrbanSubtab) =>
              rubro.companies.filter((c) => c.subcategory === sub).length;

            return (
              <div key={rubro.id} className={`rubro-accordion-item ${isOpen ? 'open' : ''}`} data-id={rubro.id}>
                <button
                  className="rubro-accordion-header"
                  type="button"
                  aria-expanded={isOpen}
                  aria-controls={bodyId}
                  onClick={() => !isSearching && setOpenId(isOpen ? null : rubro.id)}
                >
                  <span className="rubro-accordion-icon" />
                  <span className="rubro-accordion-title">{rubro.name}</span>
                  <span className="rubro-accordion-count">{countLabel}</span>
                  <span className="rubro-accordion-chevron">
                    <ChevronDown size={18} />
                  </span>
                </button>

                {/* inert: el contenido colapsado no recibe foco de teclado */}
                <div className="rubro-accordion-body" id={bodyId} inert={!isOpen}>
                  <div className="rubro-accordion-inner">
                    {rubro.id === 'urbanizaciones' && (
                      <div className="urban-subnav-bar">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={`urban-subtab-btn ${urbanSubtab === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                          onClick={() => setUrbanSubtab('all')}
                        >
                          Todos ({rubro.companies.length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={`urban-subtab-btn ${urbanSubtab === 'urbanizaciones' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                          onClick={() => setUrbanSubtab('urbanizaciones')}
                        >
                          Urbanizaciones &amp; Condominios ({countOf('urbanizaciones')})
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={`urban-subtab-btn ${urbanSubtab === 'lotes' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                          onClick={() => setUrbanSubtab('lotes')}
                        >
                          Lotes &amp; Terrenos ({countOf('lotes')})
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={`urban-subtab-btn ${urbanSubtab === 'inmobiliarias' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                          onClick={() => setUrbanSubtab('inmobiliarias')}
                        >
                          Inmobiliarias &amp; Portales ({countOf('inmobiliarias')})
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {companies.length > 0 ? (
                      <div className="companies-grid">
                        {companies.map((company) => (
                          <motion.a
                            key={`${rubro.id}-${company.name}`}
                            href={company.url}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="company-card"
                            title={`Visitar sitio de ${company.name}`}
                            whileHover={{ y: -4 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                          >
                            <div className="company-top-meta">
                              <span className="company-badge-tag">
                                {company.subcategory === 'lotes'
                                  ? 'Lotes & Terrenos'
                                  : company.subcategory === 'inmobiliarias'
                                    ? 'Inmobiliaria'
                                    : rubro.name}
                              </span>
                              <span className="company-arrow-icon">
                                <ArrowUpRight size={12} />
                              </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="company-logo-box">
                              {company.logo ? (
                                <img
                                  src={company.logo}
                                  alt={`Logo ${company.name}`}
                                  className="company-logo-img"
                                  loading="lazy"
                                  decoding="async"
                                />
                              ) : (
                                <span className="company-initial" style={{ background: company.gradient }}>
                                  {company.initial}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>

                            <div className="company-content">
                              <span className="company-acronym">{company.acronym}</span>
                              <p className="company-name">{company.name}</p>
                              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[var(--yellow-dark)] font-bold mt-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                                Visitar sitio web →
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </motion.a>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="companies-empty">
                        <p>No se encontraron empresas en esta selección.</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

````

### `src\layouts\Layout.astro`

````astro
---
import '../styles/global.css';

interface Props {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
}

const {
  title = 'EXPOINCO 2027 | 1ra Expoinmobiliaria y Construcción Internacional en El Alto',
  description = 'La 1ra Expoinmobiliaria y Construcción Internacional en El Alto, Bolivia. 150+ empresas líderes, 52 stands y rondas de negocios internacionales.',
} = Astro.props;

// Píxeles de publicidad (opcionales, configurados por variables de entorno)
const metaPixelId = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_META_PIXEL_ID ?? '';
const gtagId = import.meta.env.PUBLIC_GTAG_ID ?? '';

// Open Graph / Twitter exigen URLs absolutas (requiere `site` en astro.config.mjs).
const canonical = new URL(Astro.url.pathname, Astro.site ?? Astro.url);
const ogImage = new URL('/assets/expoinco-logo.jpg', Astro.site ?? Astro.url);
---

<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#0e1015" />
    <title>{title}</title>
    <meta name="description" content={description} />
    <link rel="canonical" href={canonical} />
    <link rel="icon" type="image/jpeg" href="/assets/expoinco-logo.jpg" />
    <link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/assets/expoinco-logo.jpg" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
    <link
      href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Anton&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600;700&family=Work+Sans:ital,wght@0,300;0,400;0,500;0,600;0,700;1,400&display=swap"
      rel="stylesheet"
    />
    <meta property="og:title" content={title} />
    <meta property="og:description" content={description} />
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta property="og:locale" content="es_BO" />
    <meta property="og:url" content={canonical} />
    <meta property="og:image" content={ogImage} />
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
    <meta name="twitter:title" content={title} />
    <meta name="twitter:description" content={description} />
    <meta name="twitter:image" content={ogImage} />
    <!-- Sin JavaScript el preloader taparía todo el sitio: se oculta. -->
    <noscript><style>.splash-screen { display: none !important; }</style></noscript>

    <!-- Meta Pixel (solo si PUBLIC_META_PIXEL_ID está configurado) -->
    {metaPixelId && (
      <script is:inline define:vars={{ pixelId: metaPixelId }}>
        !(function (f, b, e, v, n, t, s) {
          if (f.fbq) return;
          n = f.fbq = function () {
            n.callMethod ? n.callMethod.apply(n, arguments) : n.queue.push(arguments);
          };
          if (!f._fbq) f._fbq = n;
          n.push = n;
          n.loaded = !0;
          n.version = '2.0';
          n.queue = [];
          t = b.createElement(e);
          t.async = !0;
          t.src = v;
          s = b.getElementsByTagName(e)[0];
          s.parentNode.insertBefore(t, s);
        })(window, document, 'script', 'https://connect.facebook.net/en_US/fbevents.js');
        fbq('init', pixelId);
        fbq('track', 'PageView');
      </script>
    )}

    <!-- Google Ads / GA4 (solo si PUBLIC_GTAG_ID está configurado) -->
    {gtagId && (
      <>
        <script is:inline async src={`https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${gtagId}`} />
        <script is:inline define:vars={{ tagId: gtagId }}>
          window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
          function gtag() {
            window.dataLayer.push(arguments);
          }
          window.gtag = gtag;
          gtag('js', new Date());
          gtag('config', tagId);
        </script>
      </>
    )}
  </head>
  <body>
    {/* Atribución de marketing: captura UTM/click ids en el primer toque (cliente) */}
    <script>
      import { capturarUTM } from '../utils/utm';
      capturarUTM();
    </script>
    <slot />
  </body>
</html>

````

### `src\lib\adminApi.ts`

````typescript
/**
 * Cliente de la API para el panel de administración (con JWT).
 */

const API_URL: string =
  (import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL as string | undefined) ?? 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'emc_admin_token';

export function getToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}

export function setToken(token: string | null): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  if (token) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
  else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
  }
}

async function request<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    ...(options.headers as Record<string, string> | undefined),
  };
  const token = getToken();
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;

  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, { ...options, headers });
  if (res.status === 401) {
    setToken(null);
    throw new ApiError(401, 'Sesión expirada, vuelve a iniciar sesión');
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    let detail = `Error ${res.status}`;
    try {
      const body = await res.json();
      if (typeof body.detail === 'string') detail = body.detail;
    } catch {
      // cuerpo no JSON
    }
    throw new ApiError(res.status, detail);
  }
  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path),
  post: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown) =>
    request<T>(path, { method: 'POST', body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined }),
  patch: <T>(path: string, body: unknown) =>
    request<T>(path, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  put: <T>(path: string) => request<T>(path, { method: 'PUT' }),
  delete: (path: string) => request<void>(path, { method: 'DELETE' }),
};

// ---------- Auth ----------

export async function login(email: string, password: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    let detail = 'Correo o contraseña incorrectos';
    try {
      const body = await res.json();
      if (typeof body.detail === 'string') detail = body.detail;
    } catch {
      // cuerpo no JSON
    }
    throw new ApiError(res.status, detail);
  }
  const data = await res.json();
  setToken(data.access_token);
}

// ---------- Tipos ----------

export async function changeMyPassword(currentPassword: string, newPassword: string): Promise<void> {
  await request<void>('/users/me/change-password', {
````

### `src\lib\api.ts`

````typescript
/**
 * Cliente de la API FastAPI (backend/app).
 * La URL se configura con VITE_API_URL (ver .env.example).
 */

const API_URL: string =
  (import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL as string | undefined) ?? 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1';

// Diagnóstico: si el build quedó contra localhost pero se sirve desde un
// dominio público, los datos nunca se actualizarán (VITE_API_URL es de build).
if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && API_URL.includes('localhost') &&
    !['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname)) {
  console.warn(
    `[EMC] VITE_API_URL apunta a ${API_URL} pero la página corre en ${window.location.hostname}. ` +
    'Reconstruye la web con VITE_API_URL=https://tu-api/api/v1 (ver GUIA-DESPLIEGUE.md §5).',
  );
}

/** Evento global: los datos del sistema cambiaron (reserva, venta, etc.).
 * Plano, contador y paneles lo escuchan para refrescarse automáticamente. */
export const EVENTO_DATOS = 'emc:datos-actualizados';

export function notificarDatosActualizados(): void {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(EVENTO_DATOS));
  }
}

// ---------- Tipos (espejo de los schemas del backend) ----------

export interface StandPlano {
  id: string;
  codigo: string;
  tipo: string;
  estado: 'disponible' | 'bloqueado_temporal' | 'reservado' | 'vendido' | 'no_disponible';
  dimensiones: string | null;
  precio_base: number | null;
  zona_id: string;
  zona_nombre: string;
}

export interface PlanoResponse {
  zonas: { id: string; nombre: string; tipo: string; descripcion: string | null; capacidad: number | null }[];
  stands: StandPlano[];
}

export interface SolicitudReservaPayload {
  stand_id: string;
  empresa: {
    razon_social: string;
    nombre_comercial?: string | null;
    rubro?: string | null;
    ciudad?: string | null;
    pais?: string | null;
    origen?: string | null;
  };
  contacto: {
    nombre: string;
    cargo?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    whatsapp?: string | null;
    telefono?: string | null;
  };
  interes?: string | null;
}

export interface ReservaCreada {
  id: string;
  estado: string;
  fecha_expiracion: string | null;
}

// ---------- Funciones ----------

export async function fetchPlano(): Promise<PlanoResponse> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/public/plano`);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Error al consultar el plano (${res.status})`);
  return res.json();
}

export async function solicitarReserva(
  payload: SolicitudReservaPayload,
): Promise<ReservaCreada> {
  const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/reservas/solicitar`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    let detail = `Error ${res.status}`;
    try {
      const body = await res.json();
      if (typeof body.detail === 'string') detail = body.detail;
    } catch {
      // cuerpo no JSON
    }
    throw new Error(detail);
  }
  return res.json();
}

export default API_URL;
````

### `src\lib\useAutoRefresh.ts`

````typescript
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { EVENTO_DATOS } from './api';

/**
 * Refresco automático de datos en los paneles (admin/ejecutivo).
 *
 * Vuelve a cargar cuando:
 * - cambia un dato en el sistema (evento EVENTO_DATOS: reservas, ventas…),
 * - la pestaña recupera el foco,
 * - transcurre el intervalo (por defecto 30 s).
 */
export function useAutoRefresh(cargar: () => void, intervaloMs = 30000): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const alCambiar = () => cargar();
    window.addEventListener(EVENTO_DATOS, alCambiar);
    window.addEventListener('focus', alCambiar);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', alCambiar);
    const intervalo = window.setInterval(alCambiar, intervaloMs);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(EVENTO_DATOS, alCambiar);
      window.removeEventListener('focus', alCambiar);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', alCambiar);
      window.clearInterval(intervalo);
    };
  }, [cargar, intervaloMs]);
}
````

````css
.book-wrapper {
    transition:
        transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        opacity 0.3s;
    position: relative;
}

.biptico-left,
.biptico-right {

        opacity 0.3s;
}

.zoom-hint {
    color: var(--yellow);
    opacity: 0.85;
    margin-top: 2px;
    font-size: 0.58rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    display: block;
}

.splash-screen.entering-expo {
    pointer-events: none;
    transform: scale(1.03);
    opacity: 0 !important;
    transition:
        opacity 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) !important;
}

.splash-screen.entering-expo .book-wrapper {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: scale(1.04) translateY(-6px);
}

.splash-screen.entering-expo .splash-countdown,
.splash-screen.entering-expo .splash-continue-btn {
    opacity: 0;
    transition:
        opacity 0.2s,
        transform 0.2s;
    transform: translateY(8px);
}

.splash-lightbox {
    z-index: 100000;
    opacity: 0;
    visibility: hidden;
    pointer-events: none;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 16px;
    transition:
        opacity 0.28s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        visibility 0.28s;
    display: flex;
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
}

.splash-lightbox.active {
    opacity: 1;
    visibility: visible;
    pointer-events: auto;
}

.splash-lightbox-backdrop {
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
    background: #0a0a0ae0;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}

.splash-lightbox-container {
    z-index: 2;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    max-width: 92vw;
    max-height: 90vh;
    transition: transform 0.28s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    transform: scale(0.92);
}

.splash-lightbox.active .splash-lightbox-container {
    transform: scale(1);
}

.splash-lightbox-img {
    object-fit: contain;
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    border-radius: 8px;
    width: auto;
    max-width: 90vw;
    height: auto;
    max-height: 80vh;
    box-shadow:
        0 20px 60px #000000d9,
        0 0 30px #fab8004d;
}

.splash-lightbox-caption {
    color: var(--yellow);
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    background: #0e1015cc;
    border: 1px solid #fab8004d;
    border-radius: 20px;
    margin-top: 12px;
    padding: 6px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
}

.splash-lightbox-close {
    border: 1px solid var(--yellow);
    color: #fff;
    cursor: pointer;
    background: #fab80026;
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    transition:
        background 0.2s,
        transform 0.2s;
    display: flex;
    position: absolute;
    top: -44px;
    right: 0;
}

.splash-lightbox-close:hover {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

@layer base {

    *,

    :before,
    ::backdrop {

        border: 0 solid;
        margin: 0;

    code,

    sup {

        outline: auto;

    progress {

    summary {
        display: list-item;
    }

    ol,
    ul,
    menu {
        list-style: none;
    }

    img,
    svg,
    video,
    canvas,
    audio,
    iframe,
    embed,
    object {
        vertical-align: middle;
        display: block;
    }

    img,
    video {
        max-width: 100%;
        height: auto;
    }

    button,
    input,
    select,
    optgroup,
    textarea {
        font: inherit;
        font-feature-settings: inherit;
        font-variation-settings: inherit;
        letter-spacing: inherit;
        color: inherit;
        opacity: 1;
        background-color: #0000;
        border-radius: 0;
    }

    ::file-selector-button {
        font: inherit;
        font-feature-settings: inherit;
        font-variation-settings: inherit;
        letter-spacing: inherit;
        color: inherit;
        opacity: 1;
        background-color: #0000;
        border-radius: 0;
    }

    :where(select:is([multiple], [size])) optgroup {
        font-weight: bolder;
    }

    :where(select:is([multiple], [size])) optgroup option {

    ::placeholder {

    @supports (not ((-webkit-appearance: -apple-pay-button))) or (contain-intrinsic-size: 1px) {

    textarea {

    ::-webkit-search-decoration {

        padding-block: 0;

        padding-block: 0;

    ::-webkit-calendar-picker-indicator {
        line-height: 1;
    }

    :-moz-ui-invalid {
        box-shadow: none;
    }

    button,
    input:where([type="button"], [type="reset"], [type="submit"]) {
        appearance: button;
    }

    ::file-selector-button {
        appearance: button;
    }

    ::-webkit-inner-spin-button {
        height: auto;
    }

    ::-webkit-outer-spin-button {
        height: auto;
    }

    [hidden]:where(:not([hidden="until-found"])) {
````
