        --tw-ring-color: #0000000d;
    }

    @supports (color: color-mix(in lab, red, red)) {
        .ring-black\/5 {
            --tw-ring-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-black) 5%, transparent);
        }
    }

    .outline {
        outline-style: var(--tw-outline-style);
        outline-width: 1px;
    }

    .blur {
        --tw-blur: blur(8px);
        filter: var(--tw-blur, ) var(--tw-brightness, ) var(--tw-contrast, ) var(--tw-grayscale, ) var(--tw-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-invert, ) var(--tw-saturate, ) var(--tw-sepia, ) var(--tw-drop-shadow, );
    }

    .blur-md {
        --tw-blur: blur(var(--blur-md));
        filter: var(--tw-blur, ) var(--tw-brightness, ) var(--tw-contrast, ) var(--tw-grayscale, ) var(--tw-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-invert, ) var(--tw-saturate, ) var(--tw-sepia, ) var(--tw-drop-shadow, );
    }

    .contrast-125 {
        --tw-contrast: contrast(125%);
        filter: var(--tw-blur, ) var(--tw-brightness, ) var(--tw-contrast, ) var(--tw-grayscale, ) var(--tw-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-invert, ) var(--tw-saturate, ) var(--tw-sepia, ) var(--tw-drop-shadow, );
    }

    .grayscale {
        --tw-grayscale: grayscale(100%);
        filter: var(--tw-blur, ) var(--tw-brightness, ) var(--tw-contrast, ) var(--tw-grayscale, ) var(--tw-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-invert, ) var(--tw-saturate, ) var(--tw-sepia, ) var(--tw-drop-shadow, );
    }

    .filter {
        filter: var(--tw-blur, ) var(--tw-brightness, ) var(--tw-contrast, ) var(--tw-grayscale, ) var(--tw-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-invert, ) var(--tw-saturate, ) var(--tw-sepia, ) var(--tw-drop-shadow, );
    }

    .backdrop-blur {
        --tw-backdrop-blur: blur(8px);
        backdrop-filter: var(--tw-backdrop-blur, ) var(--tw-backdrop-brightness, ) var(--tw-backdrop-contrast, ) var(--tw-backdrop-grayscale, ) var(--tw-backdrop-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-invert, ) var(--tw-backdrop-opacity, ) var(--tw-backdrop-saturate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-sepia, );
    }

    .backdrop-blur-2xl {
        --tw-backdrop-blur: blur(var(--blur-2xl));
        backdrop-filter: var(--tw-backdrop-blur, ) var(--tw-backdrop-brightness, ) var(--tw-backdrop-contrast, ) var(--tw-backdrop-grayscale, ) var(--tw-backdrop-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-invert, ) var(--tw-backdrop-opacity, ) var(--tw-backdrop-saturate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-sepia, );
    }

    .backdrop-blur-3xl {
        --tw-backdrop-blur: blur(var(--blur-3xl));
        backdrop-filter: var(--tw-backdrop-blur, ) var(--tw-backdrop-brightness, ) var(--tw-backdrop-contrast, ) var(--tw-backdrop-grayscale, ) var(--tw-backdrop-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-invert, ) var(--tw-backdrop-opacity, ) var(--tw-backdrop-saturate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-sepia, );
    }

    .backdrop-filter {
        backdrop-filter: var(--tw-backdrop-blur, ) var(--tw-backdrop-brightness, ) var(--tw-backdrop-contrast, ) var(--tw-backdrop-grayscale, ) var(--tw-backdrop-hue-rotate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-invert, ) var(--tw-backdrop-opacity, ) var(--tw-backdrop-saturate, ) var(--tw-backdrop-sepia, );
    }

    .transition {
        transition-property:
            color,
            background-color,
            border-color,
            outline-color,
            text-decoration-color,
            fill,
            stroke,
            --tw-gradient-from,
            --tw-gradient-via,
            --tw-gradient-to,
            opacity,
            box-shadow,
            transform,
            translate,
            scale,
            rotate,
            filter,
            -webkit-backdrop-filter,
            backdrop-filter,
            display,
            content-visibility,
            overlay,
            pointer-events;
        transition-timing-function: var(--tw-ease,
                var(--default-transition-timing-function));
        transition-duration: var(--tw-duration, var(--default-transition-duration));
    }

    .transition-all {
        transition-property: all;
        transition-timing-function: var(--tw-ease,
                var(--default-transition-timing-function));
        transition-duration: var(--tw-duration, var(--default-transition-duration));
    }

    .transition-transform {
        transition-property: transform, translate, scale, rotate;
        transition-timing-function: var(--tw-ease,
                var(--default-transition-timing-function));
        transition-duration: var(--tw-duration, var(--default-transition-duration));
    }

    .delay-100 {
        transition-delay: 0.1s;
    }

    .delay-150 {
        transition-delay: 0.15s;
    }

    .delay-200 {
        transition-delay: 0.2s;
    }

    .duration-700 {
        --tw-duration: 0.7s;
        transition-duration: 0.7s;
    }

    .ease-\[cubic-bezier\(0\.32\,0\.72\,0\,1\)\] {
        --tw-ease: cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1);
        transition-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1);
    }

    .ease-in-out {
        --tw-ease: var(--ease-in-out);
        transition-timing-function: var(--ease-in-out);
    }

    .ease-out {
        --tw-ease: var(--ease-out);
        transition-timing-function: var(--ease-out);
    }

    @media (hover: hover) {
        .group-hover\:translate-x-1:is(:where(.group):hover *) {
            --tw-translate-x: var(--spacing);
            translate: var(--tw-translate-x) var(--tw-translate-y);
        }

        .group-hover\:-translate-y-\[1px\]:is(:where(.group):hover *) {
            --tw-translate-y: calc(1px * -1);
            translate: var(--tw-translate-x) var(--tw-translate-y);
        }

        .group-hover\:scale-105:is(:where(.group):hover *) {
            --tw-scale-x: 105%;
            --tw-scale-y: 105%;
            --tw-scale-z: 105%;
            scale: var(--tw-scale-x) var(--tw-scale-y);
        }

        .hover\:text-black:hover {
            color: var(--color-black);
        }
    }

    .active\:scale-\[0\.98\]:active {
        scale: 0.98;
    }

    @media (width>=48rem) {
        .md\:grid-cols-3 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
        }

        .md\:py-48 {
            padding-block: calc(var(--spacing) * 48);
        }

        .md\:text-5xl {
            font-size: var(--text-5xl);
            line-height: var(--tw-leading, var(--text-5xl--line-height));
        }

        .md\:text-6xl {
            font-size: var(--text-6xl);
            line-height: var(--tw-leading, var(--text-6xl--line-height));
        }

        .md\:text-7xl {
            font-size: var(--text-7xl);
            line-height: var(--tw-leading, var(--text-7xl--line-height));
        }
    }

    @media (width>=64rem) {
        .lg\:text-6xl {
            font-size: var(--text-6xl);
            line-height: var(--tw-leading, var(--text-6xl--line-height));
        }
    }

    @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
        .dark\:bg-white\/10 {
            background-color: #ffffff1a;
        }

        @supports (color: color-mix(in lab, red, red)) {
            .dark\:bg-white\/10 {
                background-color: color-mix(in oklab,
                        var(--color-white) 10%,
                        transparent);
            }
        }

        .dark\:bg-zinc-950 {
            background-color: var(--color-zinc-950);
        }

        .dark\:text-gray-100 {
            color: var(--color-gray-100);
        }
    }
}

:root {
    --yellow: #fab800;
    --yellow-light: #ffca28;
    --yellow-dark: #df9e00;
    --yellow-glow: #fab80066;
    --yellow-subtle: #fab8001f;
    --ink: #0e1015;
    --ink-card: #151821;
    --ink-border: #242936;
    --concrete: #f4f6f9;
    --concrete-dark: #e4e7ee;
    --paper: #fff;
    --steel: #64748b;
    --steel-dark: #334155;
    --line: #fab80040;
    --ok: #10b981;
    --safety: var(--yellow);
    --safety-dark: var(--yellow-dark);
    --brick: var(--yellow);
    --brick-dark: var(--yellow-dark);
    --red: var(--yellow);
    --red-dark: var(--yellow-dark);
    --blue: var(--ink);
    --blue-light: #1a1f2c;
    --blueprint: var(--yellow);
    --blueprint-light: var(--yellow-light);
}

* {
    box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
    max-width: 100vw;
    overflow-x: clip;
    scroll-padding-top: 88px;
}

body {
    background: var(--concrete);
    color: var(--ink);
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 100vw;
    margin: 0;
    font-family:
        Work Sans,
        sans-serif;
    overflow-x: clip;
}

h1,
h2,
h3,
.display {
    letter-spacing: 0.01em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 0.96;
}

.mono {
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
}

a {
    color: inherit;
}

img,
svg {
    max-width: 100%;
    display: block;
}

.wrap {
    max-width: 1180px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 0 28px;
}

section {
    position: relative;
}

.blueprint-grid {
    background-image:
        linear-gradient(var(--line) 1px, transparent 1px),
        linear-gradient(90deg, var(--line) 1px, transparent 1px);
    opacity: 0.55;
    pointer-events: none;
    background-size: 36px 36px;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}

header {
    z-index: 100;
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
    backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
    background: #0e1015e0;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #fab80066;
    transition: all 0.3s;
    animation: 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) both headerSlideDown;
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px #00000073;
}

@keyframes headerSlideDown {
    0% {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(-100%);
    }

    to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
}

.navbar {
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    max-width: 1220px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 14px 28px;
    display: flex;
}

.logo {
    color: var(--paper);
    align-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
}

.logo:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.logo-img-wrapper {
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
}

.logo-img-glow {
    opacity: 0.6;
    filter: blur(6px);
    background: radial-gradient(circle, #fab80099 0%, #0000 70%);
    border-radius: 12px;
    animation: 3s ease-in-out infinite alternate logoGlowPulse;
    position: absolute;
    inset: -3px;
}

@keyframes logoGlowPulse {
    0% {
        opacity: 0.4;
        transform: scale(0.95);
    }

    to {
        opacity: 0.85;
        transform: scale(1.15);
    }
}

.logo-img {
    object-fit: cover;
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    width: 46px;
    height: 46px;
    box-shadow: 0 0 16px var(--yellow-glow);
    z-index: 2;
    border-radius: 10px;
    flex: none;
    transition:
        transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        box-shadow 0.3s;
    position: relative;
}

.logo:hover .logo-img {
    transform: rotate(-4deg) scale(1.08);
    box-shadow: 0 0 24px #fab800cc;
}

.logo-text {
    color: var(--paper);
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    text-shadow: 0 2px 8px #00000080;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.75rem;
    line-height: 0.95;
}

.logo-text span {
    color: var(--yellow);
    text-shadow: 0 0 12px #fab80066;
}

.logo-sub {
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #c9c4b6;
    opacity: 0.9;
    margin-top: 4px;
    font-family:
        Work Sans,
        sans-serif;
    font-size: 0.6rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.navlinks-desktop {
    align-items: center;
    gap: 32px;
    display: flex;
}

nav ul {
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    list-style: none;
    display: flex;
}

nav a {
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #e2ded4;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        color 0.2s,
        background 0.2s,
        transform 0.2s;
    display: inline-block;
    position: relative;
}

nav a:after {
    content: "";
    background: var(--yellow);
    transform-origin: 50%;
    height: 2px;
    box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--yellow-glow);
    border-radius: 2px;
    transition: transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 3px;
    left: 12px;
    right: 12px;
    transform: scaleX(0);
}

nav a:hover {
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #fab8000f;
    transform: translateY(-1px);
}

nav a:hover:after {
    transform: scaleX(1);
}

.nav-cta {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg,
            var(--yellow-light) 0%,
            var(--yellow) 100%);
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border: 1px solid #fff6;
    border-radius: 6px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 10px 22px;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: all 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: inline-flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px #fab80059;
}

.nav-cta:before {
    content: "";
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #0000, #fff6, #0000);
    width: 60%;
    height: 100%;
    transition: left 0.6s;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: -100%;
    transform: skew(-20deg);
}

.nav-cta:hover {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffd54f 0%, var(--yellow-dark) 100%);
    color: #0e1015;
    transform: translateY(-2px) scale(1.03);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px #fab80099;
}

.nav-cta:hover:before {
    left: 140%;
}

.mobile-menu-btn {
    border: 1.5px solid var(--yellow);
    cursor: pointer;
    background: #fab8001f;
    border-radius: 8px;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 5px;
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    padding: 8px;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: none;
}

.mobile-menu-btn:hover {
    background: var(--yellow);
}

.hamburger-bar {
    background: #fff;
    border-radius: 2px;
    width: 100%;
    height: 2px;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: block;
}

.mobile-menu-btn:hover .hamburger-bar {
    background: #0e1015;
}

@media (width<=960px) {
    .navlinks-desktop nav {
        display: none;
    }

    .navlinks-desktop {
        gap: 10px;
    }

    .mobile-menu-btn {
        flex-shrink: 0;
        display: flex;
    }

    .nav-cta {
        white-space: nowrap;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
        padding: 8px 12px;
        font-size: 0.7rem;
    }

    .navbar {
        padding: 10px 16px;
    }

    .logo-text {
        font-size: 1.35rem;
    }

    .logo-sub {
        max-width: 140px;
        font-size: 0.52rem;
        line-height: 1.1;
    }

    .logo-img {
        width: 38px;
        height: 38px;
    }
}

@media (width<=480px) {
    .logo-sub {
        display: none;
    }

    .nav-cta span {
        font-size: 0.65rem;
    }

    .navbar {
        padding: 8px 12px;
    }

    .wrap {
        padding: 0 16px;
    }

    .hero-inner {
        padding: 24px 16px 40px !important;
    }
}

.mobile-drawer {
    z-index: 999999;
    pointer-events: none;
    visibility: hidden;
    transition: visibility 0.3s;
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
}

.mobile-drawer.open {
    pointer-events: auto;
    visibility: visible;
}

.mobile-drawer-backdrop {
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
    opacity: 0;
    background: #0a0a0acc;
    transition: opacity 0.3s;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}

.mobile-drawer.open .mobile-drawer-backdrop {
    opacity: 1;
}

.mobile-drawer-panel {
    border-left: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    background: #11141b;
    flex-direction: column;
    width: 82vw;
    max-width: 320px;
    padding: 24px 20px;
    transition: transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: flex;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    right: 0;
    overflow-y: auto;
    transform: translate(100%);
    box-shadow: -10px 0 40px #000c;
}

.mobile-drawer.open .mobile-drawer-panel {
    transform: translate(0);
}

.mobile-drawer-head {
    border-bottom: 1px solid #fab80033;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    padding-bottom: 18px;
    display: flex;
}

.mobile-drawer-close {
    border: 1px solid var(--yellow);
    color: #fff;
    cursor: pointer;
    background: #fab8001f;
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.mobile-drawer-close:hover {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
}

.mobile-nav ul {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 12px;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    list-style: none;
    display: flex;
}

.mobile-link {
    color: #f1ede1;
    background: #ffffff08;
    border: 1px solid #ffffff14;
    border-radius: 6px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    padding: 12px 14px;
    font-family:
        Work Sans,
        sans-serif;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.mobile-link span {
    color: var(--yellow);
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.mobile-link:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #fab8001f;
    transform: translate(4px);
}

.mobile-drawer-foot {
    margin-top: auto;
    padding-top: 24px;
}

.mobile-drawer-cta {
    justify-content: center;
    width: 100%;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px #fab80073;
}

.splash-screen {
    z-index: 9999;
    background: #0a0a0a;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    transition:
        opacity 0.5s,
        visibility 0.5s;
    display: flex;
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    overflow: hidden auto;
}

.splash-screen.hidden {
    opacity: 0;
    visibility: hidden;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.splash-glow {
    filter: blur(120px);
    opacity: 0.35;
    pointer-events: none;
    border-radius: 50%;
    position: absolute;
}

.splash-glow-1 {
    background: radial-gradient(circle, #fab80080, #0000 70%);
    width: 500px;
    height: 500px;
    animation: 8s ease-in-out infinite alternate glowFloat1;
    top: -10%;
    left: -8%;
}

.splash-glow-2 {
    background: radial-gradient(circle, #fab8004d, #0000 70%);
    width: 400px;
    height: 400px;
    animation: 10s ease-in-out infinite alternate glowFloat2;
    bottom: -12%;
    right: -6%;
}

@keyframes glowFloat1 {
    0% {
        transform: translate(0) scale(1);
    }

    to {
        transform: translate(40px, 30px) scale(1.15);
    }
}

@keyframes glowFloat2 {
    0% {
        transform: translate(0) scale(1);
    }

    to {
        transform: translate(-35px, -25px) scale(1.1);
    }
}

.splash-content {
    z-index: 2;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 20px;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 1040px;
    padding: 24px 20px;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
}

.splash-biptico {
    opacity: 0;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0;
    transition:
        opacity 0.9s 0.15s,
        transform 0.9s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) 0.15s;
    display: flex;
    transform: scale(0.95);
}

.splash-screen.ready .splash-biptico {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scale(1);
}

.biptico-page {
    background: #111;
    border: 2px solid #fab80073;
    border-radius: 10px;
    width: 410px;
    max-width: 44vw;
    transition:
        transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        box-shadow 0.4s,
        border-color 0.3s;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow:
        0 24px 70px #000000d9,
        0 0 35px #fab80026;
}

.biptico-left {
    transform-origin: 100%;
    border-radius: 10px 0 0 10px;
}

.biptico-right {
    transform-origin: 0;
    border-radius: 0 10px 10px 0;
}

.biptico-page:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: translateY(-6px);
    box-shadow:
        0 32px 85px #000000e6,
        0 0 45px #fab8004d;
}

.biptico-page img {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
}

.biptico-label {
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    color: #fff;
    text-align: center;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1714 0%, #0f0e0b 100%);
    border-top: 1px solid #fab80033;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.65rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.biptico-spine {
    background: linear-gradient(#2a2318 0%, #fab800 50%, #2a2318 100%);
    flex-shrink: 0;
    width: 6px;
    box-shadow: 0 0 14px #fab80066;
}

.splash-countdown {
    text-align: center;
    opacity: 0;
    transition:
        opacity 0.8s 0.4s,
        transform 0.8s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) 0.4s;
    transform: translateY(16px);
}

.splash-screen.ready .splash-countdown {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
}

.splash-cd-title {
    letter-spacing: 0.16em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #8e887d;
    margin-bottom: 6px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.65rem;
}

.splash-cd-grid {
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 8px;
    display: flex;
}

.splash-cd-cell {
    text-align: center;
    backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
    background: #fab8000f;
    border: 1px solid #fab80033;
    border-radius: 6px;
    min-width: 52px;
    padding: 6px 12px;
}

.splash-cd-num {
    color: var(--yellow);
    text-shadow: 0 0 12px #fab80059;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.45rem;
    line-height: 1.05;
}

.splash-cd-label {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #888;
    margin-top: 2px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.52rem;
}

.splash-cd-venue {
    color: #777;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    margin-top: 8px;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
}

.splash-continue-btn {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
    opacity: 0;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 50px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 13px 36px;
    font-family:
        Work Sans,
        sans-serif;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    transition: all 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: inline-flex;
    transform: translateY(18px);
    box-shadow:
        0 8px 30px #fab80066,
        0 0 #fab80000;
}

.splash-screen.ready .splash-continue-btn {
    opacity: 1;
    transition-delay: 0.55s;
    animation: 2.5s ease-in-out 1.5s infinite btnPulse;
    transform: translateY(0);
}

.splash-continue-btn:hover {
    color: #0e1015;
    background: #fff;
    animation: none;
    transform: translateY(-3px) scale(1.04);
    box-shadow:
        0 12px 40px #fab80099,
        0 0 0 6px #fab80026;
}

.splash-continue-btn:active {
    transform: translateY(0) scale(0.98);
}

.splash-continue-btn svg {
    transition: transform 0.3s;
}

.splash-continue-btn:hover svg {
    transform: translate(4px);
}

@keyframes btnPulse {

    0%,
    to {
        box-shadow:
            0 8px 30px #fab80066,
            0 0 #fab80000;
    }

    50% {
        box-shadow:
            0 8px 30px #fab80066,
            0 0 0 10px #fab80014;
    }
}

@media (width<=720px) {
    .splash-screen {
        align-items: flex-start;
        padding: 12px 10px;
    }

    .splash-content {
        gap: 12px;
        padding: 10px 6px;
    }

    .splash-biptico {
        flex-direction: row;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 4px;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 390px;
    }

    .biptico-page {
        border-radius: 6px;
        width: 48%;
        max-width: 185px;
    }

    .biptico-left {
        border-radius: 6px 0 0 6px;
    }

    .biptico-right {
        border-radius: 0 6px 6px 0;
    }

    .biptico-spine {
        width: 3px;
        height: auto;
    }

    .biptico-label {
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
        padding: 4px 6px;
        font-size: 0.5rem;
    }

    .splash-countdown {
        margin-top: 2px;
        padding: 0 4px;
    }

    .splash-cd-title {
        margin-bottom: 6px;
        font-size: 0.65rem;
    }

    .splash-cd-grid {
        gap: 6px;
    }

    .splash-cd-cell {
        min-width: 48px;
        padding: 6px 8px;
    }

    .splash-cd-num {
        font-size: 1.25rem;
    }

    .splash-cd-label {
        font-size: 0.46rem;
    }

    .splash-cd-venue {
        margin-top: 6px;
        font-size: 0.65rem;
        line-height: 1.3;
    }

    .splash-continue-btn {
        justify-content: center;
        width: 90%;
        max-width: 290px;
        margin-top: 4px;
        padding: 11px 24px;
        font-size: 0.8rem;
        box-shadow: 0 6px 20px #fab80073;
    }
}

.hero {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    padding: 70px 0 0;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.hero-diagonal {
    clip-path: polygon(30% 0, 100% 0, 100% 100%, 0% 100%);
    opacity: 0.95;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fab800 0%, #df9e00 45%, #151821 100%);
    width: 60%;
    height: 140%;
    position: absolute;
    top: -20%;
    right: -10%;
    transform: rotate(-8deg);
}

.hero-inner {
    z-index: 2;
    grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 0.85fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 40px;
    display: grid;
    position: relative;
}

.eyebrow {
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--yellow);
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-bottom: 18px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    display: inline-flex;
}

.eyebrow:before {
    content: "";
    background: var(--yellow);
    width: 22px;
    height: 2px;
}

.hero h1 {
    color: var(--paper);
    margin: 0 0 6px;
    font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 6vw, 4.6rem);
}

.hero h1 .accent {
    color: var(--yellow);
}

.hero-sub {
    color: #d8d4c8;
    max-width: 46ch;
    margin: 14px 0 26px;
    font-size: 1.05rem;
    font-weight: 500;
}

.hero-facts {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 22px;
    margin-bottom: 30px;
    display: flex;
}

.fact {
    border-left: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    padding-left: 12px;
}

.fact .k {
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #a39d8a;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.66rem;
}

.fact .v {
    color: var(--paper);
    font-size: 0.98rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.hero-ctas {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 14px;
    margin-bottom: 42px;
    display: flex;
}

.btn {
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
    border: 2px solid #0000;
    border-radius: 4px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 14px 24px;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        transform 0.15s,
        background 0.15s,
        border-color 0.15s,
        box-shadow 0.15s;
    display: inline-flex;
}

.btn:active {
    transform: translateY(1px);
}

.btn-primary {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 14px var(--yellow-glow);
    font-weight: 800;
}

.btn-primary:hover {
    background: var(--yellow-dark);
    color: #0e1015;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 6px 22px #fab8008c;
}

.btn-outline {
    color: #fff;
    background: #ffffff0d;
    border: 1.5px solid #fff6;
}

.btn-outline:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #fab8001f;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.countdown {
    z-index: 2;
    background: var(--paper);
    color: var(--ink);
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    border-radius: 4px;
    place-self: center end;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 340px;
    padding: 22px;
    position: relative;
    box-shadow: 0 12px 36px #00000073;
}

.countdown-title {
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--steel);
    justify-content: space-between;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.66rem;
    display: flex;
}

.countdown-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: 8px;
    display: grid;
}

.cd-cell {
    text-align: center;
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    background: var(--concrete);
    border-radius: 3px;
    padding: 10px 4px;
}

.cd-num {
    color: #0e1015;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.8rem;
    line-height: 1;
}

.cd-label {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--steel);
    margin-top: 4px;
    font-size: 0.6rem;
}

.countdown-venue {
    border-top: 1px dashed var(--line);
    margin-top: 14px;
    padding-top: 14px;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

@media (width<=900px) {
    .hero-inner {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .countdown {
        justify-self: stretch;
        max-width: 100%;
    }
}

.sectors {
    background: var(--paper);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
    padding: 30px 0;
}

.sector-row {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 10px;
    display: flex;
}

.sector-tag {
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ink);
    background: var(--concrete);
    border-radius: 3px;
    padding: 8px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.section-tag {
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #0e1015;
    background: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 10px var(--yellow-glow);
    border-radius: 4px;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    padding: 5px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    display: inline-block;
}

.section-head {
    max-width: 640px;
    margin-bottom: 36px;
}

.section-head h2 {
    margin: 0 0 12px;
    font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.4vw, 2.6rem);
}

.section-head p {
    color: #4b473c;
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.5;
}

.about-expo {
    color: var(--paper);
    background: #0e1015;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #fab80033;
    padding: 85px 0 60px;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.about-expo:before {
    content: "";
    background: linear-gradient(90deg,
            transparent 0%,
            var(--yellow) 50%,
            transparent 100%);
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 1200px;
    height: 1px;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 50%;
    transform: translate(-50%);
}

.about-expo .section-head h2 {
    color: #fff;
}

.about-expo .section-head p {
    color: #c9c4b6;
}

.about-featured-card {
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #151821 0%, #1c2230 100%);
    border-radius: 12px;
    margin-bottom: 32px;
    padding: 32px 36px;
    transition:
        transform 0.3s,
        box-shadow 0.3s;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow:
        0 16px 40px #0009,
        0 0 30px #fab80026;
}

.about-featured-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-4px);
    box-shadow:
        0 22px 50px #000000b3,
        0 0 40px #fab80040;
}

.about-featured-header-row {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 16px;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    display: flex;
}

.about-featured-badge {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-radius: 50px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 10px 22px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.88rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    display: inline-flex;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 14px #fab80066;
}

.legal-cert-badge {
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    background: #fab8001f;
    border-radius: 10px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    padding: 10px 22px;
    transition: all 0.3s;
    display: inline-flex;
    box-shadow:
        0 6px 20px #00000073,
        0 0 18px #fab80033;
}

.legal-cert-badge:hover {
    background: #fab80033;
    border-color: #ffd24d;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 0 26px #fab80080;
}

.legal-badge-icon {
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 8px #fab80099);
    font-size: 1.6rem;
}

.legal-badge-info {
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 3px;
    line-height: 1.3;
    display: flex;
}

.legal-badge-label {
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #d8d4c8;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.76rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.legal-badge-id {
    color: #fff;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 1.05rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.legal-badge-id strong {
    text-shadow: 0 0 12px #fab80099;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    font-size: 1.15rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: var(--yellow) !important;
}

.about-featured-title {
    color: #fff;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    text-shadow: 0 2px 6px #0009;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 2rem;
}

.about-featured-body p {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    line-height: 1.75;
    color: #fff !important;
    opacity: 1 !important;
}

.about-featured-body strong {
    text-shadow: 0 0 10px #fab80066;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fab800 !important;
}

.about-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: 20px;
    display: grid;
}

.about-card {
    background: #13161f;
    border: 1.5px solid #fab8004d;
    border-radius: 12px;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 26px 22px;
    transition:
        transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        border-color 0.3s,
        box-shadow 0.3s;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
}

.about-card:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: translateY(-5px);
    box-shadow:
        0 16px 36px #0009,
        0 0 25px #fab8004d;
}

.about-card-icon {
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    width: 52px;
    height: 52px;
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #fab8001f;
    border-radius: 10px;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    margin-bottom: 18px;
    transition:
        transform 0.3s,
        background 0.3s;
    display: flex;
    box-shadow: 0 0 18px #fab80040;
}

.about-card:hover .about-card-icon {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    transform: scale(1.08);
}

.about-card h3 {
    color: #fff;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    text-shadow: 0 2px 4px #00000080;
    margin: 0 0 14px;
    font-size: 1.45rem;
}

.about-card p {
    flex-grow: 1;
    margin: 0 0 18px;
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    line-height: 1.65;
    color: #fff !important;
    opacity: 1 !important;
}

.about-card p strong {
    text-shadow: 0 0 10px #fab80066;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fab800 !important;
}

.about-card-foot {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-top: 1px dashed #fab80066;
    padding-top: 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fab800 !important;
}

@media (width<=1024px) {
    .about-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }
}

@media (width<=640px) {
    .about-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .about-featured-card {
        padding: 24px 20px;
    }
}

.rubros {
    background: var(--concrete);
    padding: 80px 0;
}

.rubros-search-container {
    max-width: 480px;
    margin: 0 auto 32px;
    position: relative;
}

.rubros-search-input {
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: var(--paper);
    width: 100%;
    color: var(--ink);
    border-radius: 30px;
    outline: none;
    padding: 12px 18px;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    transition:
        border-color 0.2s,
        box-shadow 0.2s;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 14px #0000000a;
}

.rubros-search-input:focus {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 18px #fab80040;
}

.rubros-accordion {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    gap: 0 40px;
    max-width: 100%;
    display: grid;
}

.rubro-accordion-item {
    border-bottom: 1.5px solid #fab80033;
    flex-direction: column;
    display: flex;
}

.rubro-accordion-item:first-child,
.rubro-accordion-item:nth-child(2) {
    border-top: 1.5px solid #fab80033;
}

.rubro-accordion-item.open {
    grid-column: 1/-1;
}

.rubro-accordion-header {
    cursor: pointer;
    text-align: left;
    background: 0 0;
    border: none;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    width: 100%;
    padding: 16px 4px;
    font-family: inherit;
    transition: background 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.rubro-accordion-header:hover {
    background: #fab8000f;
}

.rubro-accordion-icon {
    border-left: 6px solid #0000;
    border-right: 6px solid #0000;
    border-top: 9px solid var(--yellow);
    flex: none;
    width: 0;
    height: 0;
    transition: transform 0.3s;
    transform: rotate(-90deg);
}

.rubro-accordion-item.open .rubro-accordion-icon {
    transform: rotate(0);
}

.rubro-accordion-title {
    color: var(--ink);
    flex: 1;
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.rubro-accordion-count {
    color: var(--steel);
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    background: #fab8001a;
    border-radius: 20px;
    padding: 3px 8px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.rubro-accordion-chevron {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    color: var(--steel);
    flex: none;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    transition:
        transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        color 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.rubro-accordion-chevron svg {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
}

.rubro-accordion-item.open .rubro-accordion-chevron {
    color: var(--yellow);
    transform: rotate(180deg);
}

.rubro-accordion-body {
    grid-template-rows: 0fr;
    transition: grid-template-rows 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: grid;
}

.rubro-accordion-item.open .rubro-accordion-body {
    grid-template-rows: 1fr;
}

.rubro-accordion-inner {
    padding: 0 4px;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.rubro-accordion-item.open .rubro-accordion-inner {
    padding: 14px 4px 28px;
    transition: padding 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.urban-subnav-bar {
    border-bottom: 1px solid #fab80033;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    padding-bottom: 14px;
    display: flex;
}

.urban-subtab-btn {
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    background: var(--paper);
    color: #4a463a;
    cursor: pointer;
    border: 1px solid #18151126;
    border-radius: 20px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 6px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    transition: all 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: inline-flex;
}

.urban-subtab-btn:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow-dark);
    color: var(--ink);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.urban-subtab-btn.active {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 12px var(--yellow-glow);
}

@media (width<=768px) {
    .rubros-accordion {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 0;
    }

    .rubro-accordion-item:nth-child(2) {
        border-top: none;
    }
}

.companies-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 16px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=1200px) {
    .companies-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        gap: 14px;
    }
}

@media (width<=760px) {
    .companies-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        gap: 12px;
    }
}

@media (width<=460px) {
    .companies-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 10px;
    }
}

.company-card {
    background: var(--paper);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ink);
    color: var(--ink);
    border-radius: 6px;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 14px;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        box-shadow 0.25s,
        border-color 0.25s;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 16px #1815110f;
}

.company-card:before {
    content: "";
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--yellow), #ffd54f);
    opacity: 0;
    height: 3px;
    transition: opacity 0.25s;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
}

.company-card:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 14px 28px var(--yellow-glow);
    transform: translateY(-6px);
}

.company-card:hover:before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.company-top-meta {
    margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.company-badge-tag {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--steel);
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.55rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.company-logo-box {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #18151114;
    border-radius: 4px;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 100%;
    height: 125px;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    padding: 10px;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px #00000005;
}

.company-logo-img {
    object-fit: contain;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 2px 4px #0000000f);
    border-radius: 2px;
    max-width: 100%;
    max-height: 100%;
    transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.company-card:hover .company-logo-img {
    transform: scale(1.08);
}

.company-initial {
    color: #fff;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    border-radius: 14px;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 72px;
    height: 72px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 2rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    display: flex;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 12px #00000026;
}

.company-card {
    cursor: pointer;
}

.company-card:hover .company-initial {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.company-arrow-icon {
    background: var(--concrete);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
    transition:
        transform 0.2s,
        background 0.2s,
        color 0.2s,
        border-color 0.2s;
    display: inline-flex;
}

.company-card:hover .company-arrow-icon {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.company-content {
    flex-direction: column;
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
}

.company-acronym {
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    margin-bottom: 4px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.15;
    transition: color 0.2s;
}

.company-card:hover .company-acronym {
    color: var(--yellow-dark);
}

.company-name {
    color: #4a463a;
    margin: 0 0 6px;
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.35;
}

.company-email {
    color: var(--steel);
    word-break: break-all;
    margin: 0;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.6rem;
    line-height: 1.3;
}

.companies-empty {
    text-align: center;
    background: #fab8000a;
    border: 1px dashed #fab8004d;
    border-radius: 8px;
    padding: 30px 20px;
}

.companies-empty p {
    color: var(--steel);
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    font-style: italic;
}

.plan {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    padding: 90px 0;
}

.plan .section-tag {
    color: #0e1015;
    background: var(--yellow);
}

.plan .section-head p {
    color: #c9c4b6;
}

.plan-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 0.9fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 36px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=900px) {
    .plan-layout {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.venue-frame {
    background: #0e0d0b;
    border: 1px solid #3a362c;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 22px;
    box-shadow: 0 12px 36px #00000080;
}

.venue-caption {
    color: #a39d8a;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    display: flex;
}

.arena-svg-container {
    background: #0a0908;
    border: 1px solid #2e2b23;
    border-radius: 4px;
    width: 100%;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.arena-map-svg {
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    user-select: none;
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
}

.stand-node {
    cursor: pointer;
    transform-origin: 50%;
    transition:
        transform 0.15s,
        filter 0.15s;
}

.stand-node .stand-box {
    fill: #161922;
    stroke: var(--yellow);
    stroke-width: 1.5px;
    rx: 4;
    ry: 4;
    transition:
        fill 0.18s,
        stroke 0.18s,
        filter 0.18s,
        transform 0.15s;
}

.stand-node .stand-num {
    fill: var(--yellow);
    text-anchor: middle;
    dominant-baseline: central;
    pointer-events: none;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        sans-serif;
    font-size: 11.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.stand-node.reserved .stand-box {
    fill: #222631;
    stroke: #475063;
    stroke-dasharray: 2 1;
}

.stand-node.reserved .stand-num {
    fill: #6e778d;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.stand-node:hover .stand-box {
    fill: var(--yellow);
    stroke: #fff;
    stroke-width: 2.2px;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px #fab800f2);
}

.stand-node:hover .stand-num {
    fill: #0e1015;
    font-weight: 800;
}

.stand-node.active .stand-box {
    fill: var(--yellow-light);
    stroke: #fff;
    stroke-width: 2.4px;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 14px #fab800);
}

.stand-node.active .stand-num {
    fill: #0e1015;
    font-weight: 800;
}

.stand-info {
    background: var(--paper);
    color: var(--ink);
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 26px 22px;
    position: static;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 30px #00000024;
}

.stand-info .stand-id {
    color: var(--yellow-dark);
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    display: block;
}

.stand-info h3 {
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--ink);
    word-break: break-word;
    margin: 4px 0 14px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.2rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 1.25;
}

.stand-info-row {
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding: 10px 0;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    display: flex;
}

.stand-info-row .k {
    color: var(--steel);
    font-weight: 600;
}

.stand-info-row .v {
    font-weight: 700;
}

.status-pill {
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    border-radius: 20px;
    padding: 3px 10px;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    display: inline-block;
}

.status-pill.free {
    color: var(--ok);
    background: #e5f2e8;
}

.status-pill.taken {
    color: var(--steel);
    background: #f1efe8;
}

.includes {
    border-top: 1px dashed var(--line);
    margin-top: 18px;
    padding-top: 18px;
}

.includes-title {
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--steel);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
}

.includes ul {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    list-style: none;
}

.includes li {
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 4px 0;
    display: flex;
}

.includes li:before {
    content: "✓";
    color: var(--ok);
    font-weight: 700;
}

.legend {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 18px;
    margin-top: 16px;
    display: flex;
}

.legend-item {
    color: #c9c4b6;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    display: flex;
}

.legend-swatch {
    border-radius: 2px;
    width: 12px;
    height: 12px;
    display: inline-block;
}

.packages {
    background: var(--paper);
    padding: 90px 0;
}

.pkg-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 22px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=900px) {
    .pkg-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.pkg-card {
    border: 2px solid var(--ink);
    background: var(--concrete);
    border-radius: 8px;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 30px 26px;
    transition:
        transform 0.25s,
        box-shadow 0.25s;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px #0000000f;
}

.pkg-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-6px);
    box-shadow: 0 16px 36px #0000001f;
}

.pkg-desc {
    color: var(--steel-dark);
    min-height: 42px;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
    line-height: 1.45;
}

.pkg-feats {
    flex-direction: column;
    flex: 1;
    gap: 10px;
    margin: 0 0 28px;
    padding: 0;
    list-style: none;
    display: flex;
}

.pkg-feats li {
    color: var(--ink);
    border-bottom: 1px solid #0000000f;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 10px;
    padding-bottom: 8px;
    font-size: 0.88rem;
    line-height: 1.4;
    display: flex;
}

.pkg-feats li:before {
    content: "✓";
    color: var(--ok);
    flex: none;
    font-weight: 800;
}

.pkg-card.featured .pkg-feats li {
    border-bottom: 1px solid #0000001f;
}

.pkg-card.featured .pkg-feats li:before {
    color: #0e1015;
}

.pkg-card .btn-outline {
    border: 2px solid var(--ink);
    color: var(--ink);
    background: #fff;
    font-weight: 800;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 12px #00000014;
}

.pkg-card .btn-outline:hover {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--yellow);
    border-color: var(--ink);
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 6px 18px #0e101540;
}

.pkg-card.featured {
    background: linear-gradient(145deg, var(--yellow) 0%, #e5a500 100%);
    color: #0e1015;
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow-dark);
    transform: translateY(-10px);
    box-shadow: 0 20px 48px #fab80066;
}

.pkg-card.featured:hover {
    transform: translateY(-14px);
    box-shadow: 0 24px 54px #fab80080;
}

.pkg-card.featured .pkg-desc {
    color: #2b261b;
    font-weight: 500;
}

.pkg-card.featured .pkg-price,
.pkg-card.featured h3 {
    color: #0e1015;
}

.pkg-card.featured .btn-primary {
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #0e1015;
    border: 2px solid #0e1015;
    box-shadow: 0 6px 18px #00000040;
}

.pkg-card.featured .btn-primary:hover {
    color: #fff;
    background: #1f232d;
    border-color: #1f232d;
}

.pkg-name {
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--yellow-dark);
    margin-bottom: 10px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.pkg-card.featured .pkg-name {
    color: #0e1015;
    font-weight: 800;
}

.pkg-price {
    color: var(--ink);
    margin: 0 0 16px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 2.6rem;
}

.pkg-cta {
    margin-top: 24px;
}

.stand-rate {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    border-radius: 6px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 24px;
    margin-top: 44px;
    padding: 26px 30px;
    display: flex;
}

.rate-price {
    color: var(--yellow);
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 2.4rem;
}

.rate-note {
    color: #c9c4b6;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
}

.expansion {
    background: var(--concrete-dark);
    padding: 90px 0;
}

.timeline {
    gap: 0;
    margin-top: 20px;
    padding-bottom: 10px;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow-x: auto;
}

.timeline:before {
    content: "";
    background: var(--line);
    height: 2px;
    position: absolute;
    top: 23px;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
}

.tl-stop {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 210px;
    padding: 44px 18px 0;
    position: relative;
}

.tl-dot {
    background: var(--paper);
    border: 3px solid var(--yellow);
    border-radius: 50%;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    position: absolute;
    top: 14px;
    left: 18px;
}

.tl-stop.current .tl-dot {
    background: var(--yellow);
    border-color: var(--yellow-dark);
    box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--yellow-glow);
}

.tl-city {
    margin-bottom: 2px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.15rem;
}

.tl-date {
    color: var(--yellow-dark);
    margin-bottom: 6px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.tl-desc {
    color: #4b473c;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
}

.sponsors {
    background: var(--concrete);
    border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
    padding: 90px 0;
    position: relative;
}

.sponsors-intro {
    text-align: center;
    max-width: 760px;
    margin: 0 auto 48px;
}

.sponsors-intro .section-tag {
    margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.sponsors-intro h2 {
    color: var(--ink);
    margin: 0 0 12px;
    font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.4vw, 2.6rem);
}

.sponsors-intro p {
    color: #4b473c;
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 1rem;
    line-height: 1.6;
}

.sponsors-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr);
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 18px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=1200px) {
    .sponsors-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        gap: 20px;
    }
}

@media (width<=760px) {
    .sponsors-grid {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        gap: 16px;
    }
}

@media (width<=460px) {
    .sponsors-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 14px;
    }
}

.sponsor-card {
    background: var(--paper);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--ink);
    color: var(--ink);
    cursor: pointer;
    border-radius: 6px;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 16px;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        box-shadow 0.25s,
        border-color 0.25s;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 16px #1815110f;
}

.sponsor-card:before {
    content: "";
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--yellow), #ffd54f);
    opacity: 0;
    height: 3px;
    transition: opacity 0.25s;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
}

.sponsor-card:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 16px 32px var(--yellow-glow);
    transform: translateY(-8px);
}

.sponsor-card:hover:before {
    opacity: 1;
}

.sponsor-top-meta {
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    display: flex;
}

.sponsor-badge-tag {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--steel);
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.58rem;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.sponsor-arrow-icon {
    background: var(--concrete);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    color: var(--ink);
    border-radius: 50%;
    flex: none;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    transition:
        background 0.2s,
        color 0.2s,
        transform 0.2s,
        border-color 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.sponsor-arrow-icon svg {
    width: 12px;
    height: 12px;
    transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.sponsor-card:hover .sponsor-arrow-icon {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.sponsor-card:hover .sponsor-arrow-icon svg {
    transform: translate(1px, -1px);
}

.sponsor-logo-box {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #18151114;
    border-radius: 4px;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 100%;
    height: 125px;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    padding: 10px;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px #00000005;
}

.sponsor-logo-box img {
    object-fit: contain;
    border-radius: 2px;
    max-width: 100%;
    max-height: 100%;
    transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.sponsor-card:hover .sponsor-logo-box img {
    transform: scale(1.08);
}

.sponsor-content {
    flex-direction: column;
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
}

.sponsor-acronym {
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    margin-bottom: 4px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 1.15rem;
    line-height: 1.1;
    transition: color 0.2s;
}

.sponsor-card:hover .sponsor-acronym {
    color: var(--yellow-dark);
}

.sponsor-name {
    color: #4a463a;
    flex: 1;
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.35;
}

.contact {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    padding: 90px 0 40px;
}

.contact .section-tag {
    color: #0e1015;
    background: var(--yellow);
}

.contact-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 40px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=820px) {
    .contact-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        gap: 36px;
    }
}

.contact h4 {
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--yellow);
    margin: 0 0 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
}

.contact-line {
    color: #d8d4c8;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 8px 0;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    display: flex;
}

.contact-link {
    color: inherit;
    align-items: center;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: inline-flex;
    position: relative;
}

.contact-link:hover {
    color: var(--yellow);
    text-underline-offset: 4px;
    text-decoration: underline;
}

.contact-email:hover,
.contact-web:hover {
    text-shadow: 0 0 10px #fab80066;
}

.venue-list div {
    margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.venue-list b {
    color: var(--paper);
    margin-bottom: 2px;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
    display: block;
}

.venue-list span {
    color: #a39d8a;
    font-size: 0.83rem;
}

.footer-bottom {
    color: #8f8a78;
    border-top: 1px solid #3a362c;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    margin-top: 60px;
    padding-top: 24px;
    font-size: 0.76rem;
    display: flex;
}

.social-row {
    gap: 12px;
    display: flex;
}

.social-dot {
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;
    color: var(--paper);
    border: 1px solid #4a4636;
    border-radius: 4px;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        border-color 0.2s,
        color 0.2s,
        background 0.2s;
    display: flex;
}

.social-dot:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    background: var(--yellow);
}

.whatsapp-float-container {
    z-index: 999;
    flex-direction: row-reverse;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    display: flex;
    position: fixed;
    bottom: 24px;
    right: 24px;
}

.whatsapp-float {
    color: #fff;
    background: #25d366;
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 58px;
    height: 58px;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition:
        transform 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        box-shadow 0.25s,
        background 0.2s;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px #25d36673;
}

.whatsapp-float:hover {
    background: #20ba59;
    transform: scale(1.08) translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 28px #25d36699;
}

.whatsapp-float:active {
    transform: scale(0.95);
}

.whatsapp-float-pulse {
    opacity: 0.8;
    pointer-events: none;
    border: 2px solid #25d366;
    border-radius: 50%;
    animation: 2.2s ease-out infinite wa-pulse;
    position: absolute;
    inset: -4px;
}

@keyframes wa-pulse {
    0% {
        opacity: 0.9;
        transform: scale(0.95);
    }

    60% {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: scale(1.35);
    }

    to {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: scale(1.35);
    }
}

.whatsapp-float svg {
    fill: #fff;
    width: 32px;
    height: 32px;
    transition: transform 0.2s;
}

.whatsapp-tooltip {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    white-space: nowrap;
    pointer-events: none;
    opacity: 0;
    border: 1px solid #ffffff26;
    border-radius: 20px;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 8px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    transition:
        opacity 0.25s,
        transform 0.25s;
    display: flex;
    transform: translate(10px);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 16px #00000040;
}

.whatsapp-float-container:hover .whatsapp-tooltip {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translate(0);
}

.whatsapp-tooltip-dot {
    background: #25d366;
    border-radius: 50%;
    width: 7px;
    height: 7px;
    display: inline-block;
    box-shadow: 0 0 6px #25d366;
}

@media (width<=600px) {
    .whatsapp-float-container {
        bottom: 18px;
        right: 18px;
    }

    .whatsapp-tooltip {
        display: none;
    }

    .whatsapp-float {
        width: 52px;
        height: 52px;
    }

    .whatsapp-float svg {
        width: 28px;
        height: 28px;
    }
}

.brochure-modal {
    z-index: 100000;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 20px;
    transition: opacity 0.35s;
    display: flex;
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
}

.brochure-modal.open {
    opacity: 1;
    pointer-events: auto;
}

.brochure-modal-overlay {
    backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
    background: #0e0d0be0;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}

.brochure-modal-container {
    z-index: 2;
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--paper);
    border: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    border-radius: 8px;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 1150px;
    max-height: 92vh;
    padding: 36px;
    transition: transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    position: relative;
    overflow-y: auto;
    transform: scale(0.94) translateY(18px);
    box-shadow: 0 24px 60px #000000bf;
}

.brochure-modal.open .brochure-modal-container {
    transform: scale(1) translateY(0);
}

.brochure-modal-close {
    color: var(--paper);
    cursor: pointer;
    background: #ffffff1a;
    border: 1px solid #fff3;
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    transition:
        background 0.2s,
        transform 0.2s,
        color 0.2s;
    display: flex;
    position: absolute;
    top: 14px;
    right: 18px;
}

.brochure-modal-close:hover {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.brochure-modal-header {
    text-align: center;
    margin-bottom: 24px;
}

.brochure-modal-header h2 {
    margin: 6px 0;
    font-size: 1.8rem;
}

.brochure-modal-header p {
    color: #c9c4b6;
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.brochure-modal-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 24px;
    display: grid;
}

@media (width<=720px) {
    .brochure-modal-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.brochure-view-card {
    background: #000;
    border: 1.5px solid #ffffff26;
    border-radius: 6px;
    flex-direction: column;
    transition:
        transform 0.25s,
        border-color 0.25s;
    display: flex;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.brochure-view-card:hover {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: translateY(-4px);
}

.brochure-view-card img {
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    display: block;
}

.brochure-view-caption {
    color: var(--paper);
    text-align: center;
    background: #14120f;
    border-top: 1px solid #ffffff1a;
    padding: 10px 14px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
}

.brands-marquee-strip {
    background: #11100d;
    border-top: 1px solid #2e2b23;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #2e2b23;
    align-items: center;
    height: 48px;
    margin-top: 36px;
    display: flex;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.marquee-label {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    white-space: nowrap;
    z-index: 2;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    align-items: center;
    height: 100%;
    padding: 0 18px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    display: flex;
    box-shadow: 4px 0 12px #0000004d;
}

.marquee-track-wrap {
    width: 100%;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.marquee-track-wrap:before,
.marquee-track-wrap:after {
    content: "";
    z-index: 2;
    pointer-events: none;
    width: 40px;
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    bottom: 0;
}

.marquee-track-wrap:before {
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, #11100d, #0000);
    left: 0;
}

.marquee-track-wrap:after {
    background: linear-gradient(-90deg, #11100d, #0000);
    right: 0;
}

.marquee-track {
    white-space: nowrap;
    will-change: transform;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 32px;
    animation: 35s linear infinite marqueeScroll;
    display: flex;
}

.marquee-track:hover {
    animation-play-state: paused;
}

@keyframes marqueeScroll {
    0% {
        transform: translate(0);
    }

    to {
        transform: translate(-50%);
    }
}

.m-item {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    color: #c9c4b6;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.76rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    transition: color 0.2s;
    display: inline-flex;
}

.m-item:hover {
    color: var(--yellow);
}

.m-dot {
    background: var(--yellow);
    opacity: 0.8;
    border-radius: 50%;
    width: 5px;
    height: 5px;
    display: inline-block;
}

.rubros-controls-wrap {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    margin-bottom: 34px;
    display: flex;
}

.rubros-search-container {
    align-items: center;
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 520px;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    display: flex;
    position: relative;
}

.rubros-search-icon {
    color: #8c8574;
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    left: 18px;
}

.rubros-search-input {
    width: 100%;
    color: var(--ink);
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #1815111a;
    border-radius: 30px;
    outline: none;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    transition: all 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    box-shadow: 0 4px 20px #00000008;
    padding: 14px 44px 14px 48px !important;
}

.rubros-search-input:focus {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px #fab80033;
}

.rubros-clear-btn {
    color: #8c8574;
    cursor: pointer;
    background: 0 0;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 50%;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 4px;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    transition: color 0.2s;
    display: flex;
    position: absolute;
    right: 14px;
}

.rubros-clear-btn:hover {
    color: var(--ink);
}

.rubros-pills-row {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 10px;
    display: flex;
}

.rubro-pill {
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    color: #5d5747;
    cursor: pointer;
    white-space: nowrap;
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #18151114;
    border-radius: 24px;
    padding: 9px 16px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    transition: all 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px #00000005;
}

.rubro-pill:hover {
    color: #0e1015;
    border-color: #1815114d;
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    box-shadow: 0 6px 14px #0000000a;
}

.rubro-pill.active {
    color: var(--yellow);
    background: #0e1015;
    border-color: #0e1015;
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    box-shadow: 0 6px 16px #0e10152e;
}

.stands-filter-group {
    background: #181511;
    border: 1px solid #3a362c;
    border-radius: 20px;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 3px;
    display: inline-flex;
}

.stand-filter-btn {
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: #a39d8a;
    cursor: pointer;
    background: 0 0;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 14px;
    padding: 4px 10px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.65rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    transition: all 0.18s;
}

.stand-filter-btn:hover {
    color: var(--paper);
}

.stand-filter-btn.active {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: #0e1015;
    font-weight: 800;
}

.stand-node.dimmed {
    opacity: 0.2;
    filter: grayscale();
    pointer-events: none;
}

.stand-hover-tooltip {
    z-index: 50;
    pointer-events: none;
    border: 1px solid var(--yellow);
    color: var(--paper);
    white-space: nowrap;
    background: #0e1015f2;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    transition: opacity 0.12s;
    position: absolute;
    transform: translate(-50%, -130%);
    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px #0009;
}

.stand-hover-tooltip strong {
    color: var(--yellow);
}

header {
    transition:
        background 0.3s,
        -webkit-backdrop-filter 0.3s,
        backdrop-filter 0.3s,
        border-color 0.3s,
        box-shadow 0.3s;
}

header.scrolled {
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(14px) !important;
    background: #0e1015e0 !important;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #fab80040 !important;
    box-shadow: 0 8px 32px #00000059 !important;
}

.reveal {
    opacity: 0;
    will-change: opacity, transform;
    transition:
        opacity 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1),
        transform 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    transform: translateY(32px);
}

.reveal.revealed {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
}

.impact-stats-strip {
    background: #14120f;
    border: 1px solid #332f26;
    border-radius: 8px;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: 16px;
    margin: 36px 0;
    padding: 26px 20px;
    display: grid;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 30px #0000004d;
}

@media (width<=850px) {
    .impact-stats-strip {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        gap: 20px;
    }
}

@media (width<=480px) {
    .impact-stats-strip {
        text-align: center;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
}

.stat-box {
    text-align: center;
    border-right: 1px solid #2a261e;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    display: flex;
}

.stat-box:last-child {
    border-right: none;
}

@media (width<=850px) {
    .stat-box:nth-child(2) {
        border-right: none;
    }
}

.stat-num-row {
    color: var(--yellow);
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: baseline;
    margin-bottom: 6px;
    font-family: Anton, sans-serif;
    font-size: 2.8rem;
    line-height: 1;
    display: flex;
}

.stat-plus {
    color: var(--paper);
    margin-left: 2px;
    font-size: 2rem;
}

.stat-label {
    color: #c9c4b6;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    font-family:
        JetBrains Mono,
        monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.glow-card {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.glow-card:after {
    content: "";
    top: var(--glow-y, -1000px);
    left: var(--glow-x, -1000px);
    pointer-events: none;
    opacity: 0;
    background: radial-gradient(circle, #fab8001f 0%, #0000 70%);
    width: 280px;
    height: 280px;
    transition: opacity 0.3s;
    position: absolute;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
}

.glow-card:hover:after {
    opacity: 1;
}
