````css
/* ---- Anclas: que el header sticky no tape el título de cada sección ---- */
section[id] {
    scroll-margin-top: 88px;
}

/* ---- Foco visible para navegación con teclado ---- */
:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    outline-offset: 3px;
}

.stand-node:focus-visible {
    outline: none;
}

.stand-node:focus-visible .stand-box {
    stroke: #fff;
    stroke-width: 2.4px;
}

/* ---- Respeta "reducir movimiento" del sistema ---- */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    html {
        scroll-behavior: auto;
    }

    *,
    *::before,
    *::after {
        animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
        animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
        transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    }

    .marquee-track {
        animation: none !important;
    }
}

/* ===== Modal de reserva de stands (ReservaModal) ===== */
.reserva-form {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
}
.reserva-form fieldset {
    border: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.75rem;
}
.reserva-form legend {
    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    color: var(--muted, #8f9092);
    margin-bottom: 0.35rem;
}
.reserva-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
}
.reserva-field {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    color: var(--text, #edeae2);
}
.reserva-field input {
    padding: 0.65rem 0.85rem;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
    color: inherit;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
    outline: none;
    transition: border-color 0.2s;
}
.reserva-field input:focus {
    border-color: var(--safety, #f6b73c);
}
.reserva-grid .span-2 {
    grid-column: span 2;
}
.reserva-error {
    color: #ff8f8f;
    font-size: 0.88rem;
    margin: 0;
}
.reserva-success {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.9rem;
}
@media (max-width: 640px) {
    .reserva-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    .reserva-grid .span-2 {
        grid-column: span 1;
    }
}

/* ===== Panel de administración (/admin) ===== */
/* ================= PANEL ADMIN / EJECUTIVO =================
   Sistema de diseño unificado con el sitio público (tokens :root):
   fondo --ink, tarjetas --ink-card, bordes --ink-border, acento --yellow.
   Contraste AA: texto claro sobre oscuro; texto oscuro sobre amarillo. */

/* --- Login --- */
.admin-login-wrap {
    min-height: 100vh;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 1.5rem;
    background: var(--ink);
}
.admin-login-card {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: 420px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 2.25rem 2rem;
    border-radius: 18px;
    background: var(--ink-card);
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    box-shadow: 0 24px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
}
.admin-login-card h1 {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 1.45rem;
    color: var(--concrete);
}
.admin-login-card p {
    margin: 0;
    color: #a9b1c2;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
}

/* --- Layout base --- */
.admin-shell {
    display: flex;
    min-height: 100vh;
    background: var(--ink);
    color: var(--concrete);
}

/* --- Barra superior (solo móvil) --- */
.admin-topbar {
    display: none;
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 40;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    padding: 0.7rem 1rem;
    background: var(--ink-card);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
}
.admin-menu-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    border-radius: 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
    color: var(--concrete);
    font-size: 1.15rem;
    cursor: pointer;
}
.admin-menu-btn:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--yellow);
}

/* --- Sidebar --- */
.admin-sidebar {
    width: 262px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1rem;
    padding: 1.35rem 1rem 1.1rem;
    background: var(--ink-card);
    border-right: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    height: 100vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    scrollbar-width: thin;
}
.admin-brand {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    padding: 0 0.55rem;
}
.admin-brand strong {
    font-size: 1.06rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    color: var(--concrete);
}
.admin-brand .section-tag {
    align-self: flex-start;
}
.admin-nav {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.15rem;
    flex: 1;
}
.admin-nav-group {
    margin: 0.85rem 0 0.3rem;
    padding: 0 0.55rem;
    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    font-size: 0.62rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #8b95a8;
}
.admin-nav-group:first-child {
    margin-top: 0;
}
.admin-nav-btn {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.65rem;
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.62rem 0.75rem;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: transparent;
    color: #c9d1de;
    font-size: 0.885rem;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.18s, color 0.18s;
}
.admin-nav-btn:hover {
    background: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.1);
    color: var(--concrete);
}
.admin-nav-btn:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    outline-offset: 1px;
}
/* Activo: amarillo de marca con texto tinta → contraste ~11:1 */
.admin-nav-btn.active {
    background: var(--yellow);
    color: var(--ink);
    font-weight: 700;
}
.admin-sidebar-footer {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.6rem;
    padding-top: 0.85rem;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: #a9b1c2;
}
.admin-sidebar-footer small {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.25rem;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}
.admin-logout-btn {
    padding: 0.55rem 0.85rem;
    border-radius: 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
    color: var(--concrete);
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.18s, border-color 0.18s;
}
.admin-logout-btn:hover {
    background: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.14);
    border-color: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.5);
    color: #fecaca;
}

/* --- Contenido principal --- */
.admin-main {
    flex: 1;
    padding: clamp(1.1rem, 3vw, 2.25rem);
    min-width: 0;
}
.admin-section {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1.25rem;
    max-width: 1280px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    width: 100%;
}
.admin-section-head {
    padding-bottom: 1rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
}
.admin-section-head h2 {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: clamp(1.35rem, 2.5vw, 1.7rem);
    color: var(--concrete);
}
.admin-section-head p {
    margin: 0.4rem 0 0;
    color: #a9b1c2;
    font-size: 0.92rem;
}
.admin-loading {
    color: #a9b1c2;
}
.admin-muted {
    color: #a9b1c2;
    font-size: 0.88rem;
}
.admin-ok {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0.7rem 1rem;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.12);
    border: 1px solid rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.35);
    color: #86efac;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
}

/* --- Alertas --- */
.admin-alerts {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.5rem;
}
.admin-alert {
    padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.1);
    border: 1px solid rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.35);
    color: #fcd34d;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
}
.admin-alert.alert-seguimiento {
    background: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.35);
    color: #93c5fd;
}
.admin-alert.alert-reserva {
    background: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.35);
    color: #fcd34d;
}
.admin-alert.alert-asignacion {
    background: rgba(167, 139, 250, 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(167, 139, 250, 0.35);
    color: #c4b5fd;
}
.admin-alert.alert-inventario {
    background: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.35);
    color: #86efac;
}
.admin-alert.alert-alerta {
    background: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.1);
    border-color: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.35);
    color: #fca5a5;
}

/* --- Tarjetas --- */
.admin-cards {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(215px, 1fr));
    gap: 1rem;
}
.admin-card {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    padding: 1.25rem;
    border-radius: 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: var(--ink-card);
    transition: transform 0.18s, border-color 0.18s;
}
.admin-card:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
    border-color: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.35);
}
.admin-card.accent {
    border-color: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.55);
    background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.1), rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.02)), var(--ink-card);
}
.admin-card .lbl {
    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    color: #a9b1c2;
}
.admin-card .big {
    font-size: 1.9rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fff;
    line-height: 1.15;
}
.admin-card .sub {
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    color: #a9b1c2;
}

/* --- Paneles y rejillas --- */
.admin-grid-2 {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr));
    gap: 1rem;
}
.admin-panel {
    padding: 1.25rem;
    border-radius: 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: var(--ink-card);
}
.admin-panel h3 {
    margin: 0 0 0.85rem;
    font-size: 1.02rem;
    color: var(--concrete);
}

/* --- Tablas --- */
.admin-table-wrap {
    overflow-x: auto;
    border-radius: 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: var(--ink-card);
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
}
.admin-table {
    width: 100%;
    border-collapse: collapse;
    font-size: 0.88rem;
}
.admin-table th,
.admin-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.9rem;
    text-align: left;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: #dde3ec;
}
.admin-table th {
    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    font-size: 0.66rem;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    color: #a9b1c2;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.035);
}
.admin-table tbody tr:hover td {
    background: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.05);
}
.admin-table tr:last-child td {
    border-bottom: none;
}
.admin-table a {
    color: var(--yellow);
}

/* --- Toolbars y formularios --- */
.admin-toolbar {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    align-items: center;
}
.admin-form-inline,
.admin-form-grid {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    align-items: center;
}
.admin-form-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(230px, 1fr));
    align-items: end;
}
.admin-input {
    padding: 0.65rem 0.85rem;
    border-radius: 10px;
    border: 1px solid #2a3242;
    background: #0f131b;
    color: var(--concrete);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    outline: none;
    min-width: 180px;
    transition: border-color 0.18s, box-shadow 0.18s;
}
.admin-input:focus {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.18);
}
.admin-input::placeholder {
    color: #77839b;
}
/* Opciones de selects: fondo oscuro con texto claro (móvil/desktop) */
.admin-input option,
.admin-select-mini option {
    background: var(--ink-card);
    color: var(--concrete);
}
.admin-select-mini {
    padding: 0.45rem 0.55rem;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid #2a3242;
    background: #0f131b;
    color: var(--concrete);
    font-size: 0.82rem;
}

/* --- Botones pequeños --- */
.admin-btn-mini {
    padding: 0.38rem 0.7rem;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid #2f3849;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
    color: #dfe5ee;
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.18s, border-color 0.18s, color 0.18s;
}
.admin-btn-mini:hover {
    background: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.14);
    border-color: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.5);
    color: var(--yellow);
}
.admin-btn-mini:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--yellow);
    outline-offset: 1px;
}
.admin-btn-mini.danger {
    border-color: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.4);
    color: #fca5a5;
}
.admin-btn-mini.danger:hover {
    background: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.16);
    border-color: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.6);
}
.admin-btn-mini.success {
    border-color: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.4);
    color: #86efac;
}
.admin-btn-mini.success:hover {
    background: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.16);
    border-color: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.6);
}
.admin-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: 0.4rem;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}

/* --- Pills de estado/prioridad (contraste AA) --- */
.admin-pill {
    display: inline-block;
    padding: 0.22rem 0.62rem;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    color: #dfe5ee;
}
/* rojo: urgencia / error */
.admin-pill.prio-alta,
.admin-pill.estado-error,
.admin-pill.estado-expirado,
.admin-pill.estado-cancelado,
.admin-pill.estado-anulada,
.admin-pill.estado-descartado {
    background: rgba(248, 113, 113, 0.16);
    color: #fca5a5;
}
/* ámbar: atención / en curso */
.admin-pill.prio-media,
.admin-pill.estado-bloqueado,
.admin-pill.estado-bloqueado_temporal,
.admin-pill.estado-pendiente,
.admin-pill.estado-borrador,
.admin-pill.estado-reserva_temporal {
    background: rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.16);
    color: #fcd34d;
}
/* verde: éxito / disponible */
.admin-pill.prio-baja,
.admin-pill.estado-disponible,
.admin-pill.estado-activo,
.admin-pill.estado-vendido,
.admin-pill.estado-emitida,
.admin-pill.estado-confirmado,
.admin-pill.estado-finalizada {
    background: rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.16);
    color: #86efac;
}
/* azul: proceso activo */
.admin-pill.estado-reservado,
.admin-pill.estado-enviado,
.admin-pill.estado-contactado,
.admin-pill.estado-interesado,
.admin-pill.estado-propuesta_enviada,
.admin-pill.estado-en_negociacion {
    background: rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.16);
    color: #93c5fd;
}
/* violeta: leído/completado */
.admin-pill.estado-leido,
.admin-pill.estado-entregado,
.admin-pill.estado-respondido,
.admin-pill.estado-aprobada,
.admin-pill.estado-enviando {
    background: rgba(167, 139, 250, 0.16);
    color: #c4b5fd;
}

/* --- Embudo --- */
.admin-funnel {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.5rem;
}
.admin-funnel-step {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.2rem;
    padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem;
    border-radius: 12px;
    border: 1px solid var(--ink-border);
    background: var(--ink-card);
    color: inherit;
    cursor: pointer;
    min-width: 96px;
    transition: border-color 0.18s, background 0.18s;
}
.admin-funnel-step .n {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fff;
}
.admin-funnel-step .l {
    font-size: 0.66rem;
    text-transform: capitalize;
    color: #a9b1c2;
}
.admin-funnel-step:hover {
    border-color: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.4);
}
.admin-funnel-step.active {
    border-color: var(--yellow);
    background: rgba(250, 184, 0, 0.1);
}

/* --- Responsive: drawer lateral en pantallas medianas/pequeñas --- */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    .admin-shell {
        flex-direction: column;
    }
    .admin-topbar {
        display: flex;
    }
    .admin-sidebar {
        position: fixed;
        z-index: 50;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        height: 100vh;
        transform: translateX(-105%);
        transition: transform 0.25s ease;
        box-shadow: 12px 0 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
    }
    .admin-sidebar.open {
        transform: translateX(0);
    }
    .admin-sidebar-backdrop {
        position: fixed;
        inset: 0;
        z-index: 45;
        background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);
    }
}
@media (max-width: 640px) {
    .admin-main {
        padding: 1rem 0.85rem;
    }
    .admin-cards {
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
    }
    .admin-form-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
    .admin-input {
        min-width: 0;
        width: 100%;
    }
    .admin-card .big {
        font-size: 1.55rem;
    }
    .admin-table th,
    .admin-table td {
        padding: 0.65rem 0.7rem;
        font-size: 0.82rem;
    }
}

/* ---------- Contador de ocupación en vivo (LiveStats) ---------- */
.live-stats {
    padding: 1.25rem 0;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(46, 125, 50, 0.12), rgba(27, 94, 32, 0.06));
    border-top: 1px solid rgba(46, 125, 50, 0.25);
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(46, 125, 50, 0.25);
}
.live-stats-row {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 1rem 1.5rem;
    justify-content: space-between;
}
.live-stats-badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    color: #a5d6a7;
}
.live-stats-pulse {
    width: 10px;
    height: 10px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #43a047;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.6);
    animation: live-pulse 1.8s infinite;
}
@keyframes live-pulse {
    0% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.6); }
    70% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 10px rgba(67, 160, 71, 0); }
    100% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(67, 160, 71, 0); }
}
.live-stats-numbers {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0.5rem 1.5rem;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
}
.live-stats-numbers strong {
    font-size: 1.15rem;
}
.live-stats-cta {
    white-space: nowrap;
}
@media (max-width: 860px) {
    .live-stats-row { justify-content: center; text-align: center; }
}

````

### `src\utils\countdown.ts`

````typescript
/**
 * Inicio oficial de la expo: 21 de abril de 2027, 09:00 hora de Bolivia (UTC-4).
 * Se usa formato ISO con offset para que el resultado sea idéntico en cualquier
 * zona horaria y navegador (el formato "April 21, 2027" depende del navegador).
 */
export const EXPO_START = new Date('2027-04-21T09:00:00-04:00').getTime();

interface CountdownIds {
  days: string;
  hours: string;
  mins: string;
  secs: string;
}

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

function setText(id: string, value: string) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.textContent = value;
}

/** Inicia una cuenta regresiva y la detiene sola al llegar a cero. */
export function startCountdown(ids: CountdownIds, onEnd?: () => void) {
  let timer: number | undefined;

  const tick = () => {
    const diff = Math.max(0, EXPO_START - Date.now());

    setText(ids.days, pad(Math.floor(diff / 86_400_000)));
    setText(ids.hours, pad(Math.floor((diff % 86_400_000) / 3_600_000)));
    setText(ids.mins, pad(Math.floor((diff % 3_600_000) / 60_000)));
    setText(ids.secs, pad(Math.floor((diff % 60_000) / 1000)));

    if (diff === 0) {
      if (timer !== undefined) window.clearInterval(timer);
      onEnd?.();
    }
  };

  tick();
  timer = window.setInterval(tick, 1000);
}

````

### `src\utils\utm.ts`

````typescript
/**
 * Atribución de marketing (quick win 2 — INFORME.md §11).
 *
 * - Captura parámetros UTM y click ids (gclid, fbclid) de la URL en el primer
 *   toque y los conserva en localStorage (atribución de primer contacto).
 * - `getFuente()` devuelve una cadena compacta (≤50 chars) que el backend
 *   guarda como `origen` del lead/empresa.
 * - `track()` dispara eventos a Meta Pixel / Google (gtag) si están instalados.
 */

const STORAGE_KEY = 'emc_fuente';

function leerUTM(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

  const source = params.get('utm_source');
  const medium = params.get('utm_medium');
  const campaign = params.get('utm_campaign');
  const gclid = params.get('gclid');
  const fbclid = params.get('fbclid');

  let fuente: string | null = null;
  if (source) {
    fuente = [source, medium, campaign].filter(Boolean).join('/');
  } else if (gclid) {
    fuente = 'google/cpc';
  } else if (fbclid) {
    fuente = 'meta/cpc';
  }
  return fuente ? fuente.slice(0, 50) : null;
}

/** Captura la fuente de la URL (primer toque) y la conserva. */
export function capturarUTM(): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existente = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    const nueva = leerUTM();
    if (nueva && !existente) {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, nueva);
    }
  } catch {
    // almacenamiento no disponible: la atribución simplemente se omite
  }
}

/** Fuente compacta para los formularios (p. ej. "meta/cpc/verano27"). */
export function getFuente(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    return window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) ?? leerUTM();
  } catch {
    return leerUTM();
  }
}

type VentanaAnalitica = Window & {
  gtag?: (...args: unknown[]) => void;
  fbq?: (...args: unknown[]) => void;
};

/** Registra un evento de marketing si Meta Pixel / Google (gtag) están cargados. */
export function track(evento: string, datos: Record<string, unknown> = {}): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  const w = window as VentanaAnalitica;
  try {
    if (typeof w.gtag === 'function') {
      w.gtag('event', evento, { ...datos, source: getFuente() ?? 'directo' });
    }
    if (typeof w.fbq === 'function') {
      w.fbq('track', evento, { ...datos, source: getFuente() ?? 'directo' });
    }
  } catch {
    // la analítica nunca debe romper la experiencia
  }
}

````
